double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    //            threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < i < N; ++i) {\n        nl.offsets[i] = total;\n        total += counts[i];\n    }\n    nl.offsets[N] = total;\n\n    static const size_t MAX_NEIGHBOR_ENTRIES = size_t(1) << 28; // 1 GiB of ints\n    if (total > MAX_NEIGHBOR_ENTRIES) {\n        nl.offsets.clear();\n        return nl;\n    }\n\n    nl.nbrs.resize(total);\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 64)\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        size_t pos = nl.offsets[i];\n        const Point p = points[i];\n        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            if (j != i && distance(points[j], p) < threshold) nl.nbrs[pos++] = j;\n        }\n    }\n    nl.available = true;\n    return nl;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    const NeighborLists nl = buildNeighborLists(points, threshold);\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n    std::vector<int> thread_best_card(nthreads);\n    std::vector<int> thread_best_pos(nthreads);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed (in parallel); the winner is the\n        // seed with maximum cardinality, earliest in the list on ties\n        #pragma omp parallel num_threads(nthreads)\n        {\n            const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n            CandidateWorkspace& ws = workspaces[tid];\n            if (static_cast<int>(ws.idx.size()) < N) ws.reserve(N);\n            int best_card = -1;\n            int best_pos = -1;\n\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1) nowait\n            for (int i = 0; i < U; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,\n                                                                 threshold, N, nl, ws);\n                if (cardinality > best_card) {\n                    best_card = cardinality;\n                    best_pos = i;\n                }\n            }\n            thread_best_card[tid] = best_card;\n            thread_best_pos[tid] = best_pos;\n        }\n\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_pos = -1;\n        for (int t = 0; t < nthreads; ++t) {\n            if (thread_best_pos[t] < 0) continue;\n            if (thread_best_card[t] > max_cardinality ||\n                (thread_best_card[t] == max_cardinality && thread_best_pos[t] < best_pos)) {\n                max_cardinality = thread_best_card[t];\n                best_pos = thread_best_pos[t];\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_pos >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            const int best_seed = unclustered_indices[best_pos];\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, threshold, N, nl,\n                                     workspaces[0], &cluster.members);\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[cluster.members[i]] = 1;\n            }\n            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <omp.h>\\n',1)\nsrc=src.replace('// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version','// QT Clustering Benchmark - OpenMP Parallel Version')\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)\n\nproject(qtclustering)\n\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)\n\nfind_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)\n\nadd_executable(qtclustering qtclustering.cpp)\n\ntarget_compile_options(qtclustering PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)\n\n# Link OpenMP and math library\ntarget_link_libraries(qtclustering OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX m)\nEOF\nmkdir -p /tmp/qtb && cd /tmp/qtb && cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release /home/llmtest/evals/20261002-142720/qtclustering_claude-opus-5.5-cc-medium_omp_r5/qtclustering >/dev/null && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning|Built\"","uuid":"9cf50387-28fa-4c3e-b74c-3896c0d387a4","session_id":"a3f50c3f-2cc0-48d1-b77a-bf76eaa4a696"}

Seeds are processed in parallel.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    const NeighborLists nl = buildNeighborLists(points, threshold);\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n    std::vector<int> card_cache(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> cache_valid(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> dirty, bounds, order;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        // Collect seeds whose cached cardinality is stale; best cached cardinality\n        dirty.clear();\n        int global_best = -1;\n        for (int i = 0; i < U; ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (cache_valid[seed]) {\n                global_best = std::max(global_best, card_cache[seed]);\n            } else {\n                dirty.push_back(seed);\n            }\n        }\n        const int D = static_cast<int>(dirty.size());\n\n        // Upper bound on each stale seed's cardinality: the seed plus all\n        // unclustered points within the threshold. Stale seeds are processed in\n        // decreasing bound order so that seeds which cannot reach the best\n        // cardinality found so far can be skipped (does not change the result).\n        bounds.resize(D);\n        order.resize(D);\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 64) num_threads(nthreads)\n        for (int i = 0; i < D; ++i) {\n            const int seed = dirty[i];\n            const Point sp = points[seed];\n            int c = 1;\n            if (nl.available) {\n                for (size_t k = nl.offsets[seed]; k < nl.offsets[seed + 1]; ++k) {\n                    c += clustered[nl.nbrs[k]] ? 0 : 1;\n                }\n            } else {\n                for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n                    c += (j != seed && !clustered[j] && distance(points[j], sp) < threshold) ? 1 : 0;\n                }\n            }\n            bounds[i] = c;\n            order[i] = i;\n        }\n        std::sort(order.begin(), order.end(), [&](int a, int b) {\n            return bounds[a] != bounds[b] ? bounds[a] > bounds[b] : a < b;\n        });\n\n        // Compute cardinalities of stale seeds in parallel\n        #pragma omp parallel num_threads(nthreads)\n        {\n            CandidateWorkspace& ws = workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()];\n            if (static_cast<int>(ws.idx.size()) < N) ws.reserve(N);\n\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int k = 0; k < D; ++k) {\n                const int i = order[k];\n                int gb;\n                #pragma omp atomic read relaxed\n                gb = global_best;\n                if (bounds[i] < gb) continue; // cannot be the best; stays stale\n\n                const int seed = dirty[i];\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,\n                                                                 threshold, N, nl, ws);\n                card_cache[seed] = cardinality;\n                cache_valid[seed] = 1;\n                if (cardinality > gb) {\n                    #pragma omp atomic compare relaxed\n                    if (global_best < cardinality) { global_best = cardinality; }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // The winner is the seed with maximum cardinality, earliest on ties\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        for (int i = 0; i < U; ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (cache_valid[seed] && card_cache[seed] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = card_cache[seed];\n                best_seed = seed;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, threshold, N, nl,\n                                     workspaces[0], &cluster.members);\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[cluster.members[i]] = 1;\n            }\n\n            // Invalidate cached cardinalities of seeds near the new cluster\n            if (nl.available) {\n                for (const int mbr : cluster.members) {\n                    for (size_t k = nl.offsets[mbr]; k < nl.offsets[mbr + 1]; ++k) {\n                        cache_valid[nl.nbrs[k]] = 0;\n                    }\n                }\n            } else {\n                const std::vector<int>& mbrs = cluster.members;\n                const int M = static_cast<int>(mbrs.size());\n                #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(nthreads)\n                for (int i = 0; i < U; ++i) {\n                    const int j = unclustered_indices[i];\n                    if (clustered[j] || !cache_valid[j]) continue;\n                    const Point pj = points[j];\n                    for (int k = 0; k < M; ++k) {\n                        if (distance(points[mbrs[k]], pj) < threshold) {\n                            cache_valid[j] = 0;\n                            break;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncd /tmp/qtb && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning|Built\"; Larger inputs would take too long with the original, so I checked them against two test builds of my own code: one with the caching turned off and one using the fallback path. All three gave identical hashes for 20000 points, 5000 points at `-t 0.5` and `-t 5`, and 7000 points at `-t 1`.\n\n**What changed in `qtclustering.cpp`:**\n- **Parallel seeds:** each round tries every remaining seed point in parallel. The winner is chosen exactly as before: the largest cluster, with ties going to the lowest point index.\n- **Faster cluster growth:** each candidate point keeps a running \"farthest distance to the cluster so far\", updated only against the newly added point. The original recomputed this against every member each time. Points that reach the threshold are dropped for good, since that distance can only grow. Ties still go to the lowest index, and distances are computed the same way, so results are unchanged.\n- **Neighbour lists:** for each point, the program builds a list of points within the threshold, in parallel. If these would take more than about 1 GiB, it falls back to scanning all points.\n- **Caching:** a seed's cluster depends only on the unclustered points near it. Its size is stored and only recomputed when one of those neighbours joins a cluster, so each round only redoes the seeds near the cluster just removed.\n- **Pruning:** seeds whose upper bound (1 + unclustered neighbours) is below the best size found so far are skipped, which can't change the result. Seeds with the largest bounds are processed first so this happens more often.\n- **Small workloads:** parallel regions with very little work run on one thread to avoid overhead.\n\n`CMakeLists.txt` now requires OpenMP (`find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)`) and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. The executable name is unchanged, and it builds with no warnings.\n\n**Timings** (default threshold of 2.0; the 128-core, 256-thread machine was shared, with load averages of about 18–42, so these are noisy):\n\n| Points | 1 thread | 8 | 32 | 256 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 20000 | 26.6 s | 3.6 s | 1.1 s | 0.58 s |\n| 50000 | 322 s | 41.5 s | 11.5 s | 3.9 s |\n\nThese figures include the algorithm changes, not just the parallelism. One weak spot: very small inputs, or ones that produce many tiny clusters, can be slower at 256 threads than at 8–32. Larger inputs would take too long with the original, so I checked them against two test builds of my own code: one with the caching turned off and one using the fallback path. All three gave identical hashes for 20000 points, 5000 points at `-t 0.5` and `-t 5`, and 7000 points at `-t 1`.\n\n**What changed in `qtclustering.cpp`:**\n- **Parallel seeds:** each round tries every remaining seed point in parallel. The winner is chosen exactly as before: the largest cluster, with ties going to the lowest point index.\n- **Faster cluster growth:** each candidate point keeps a running \"farthest distance to the cluster so far\", updated only against the newly added point. The original recomputed this against every member each time. Points that reach the threshold are dropped for good, since that distance can only grow. Ties still go to the lowest index, and distances are computed the same way, so results are unchanged.\n- **Neighbour lists:** for each point, the program builds a list of points within the threshold, in parallel. If these would take more than about 1 GiB, it falls back to scanning all points.\n- **Caching:** a seed's cluster depends only on the unclustered points near it. Its size is stored and only recomputed when one of those neighbours joins a cluster, so each round only redoes the seeds near the cluster just removed.\n- **Pruning:** seeds whose upper bound (1 + unclustered neighbours) is below the best size found so far are skipped, which can't change the result. Seeds with the largest bounds are processed first so this happens more often.\n- **Small workloads:** parallel regions with very little work run on one thread to avoid overhead.\n\n`CMakeLists.txt` now requires OpenMP (`find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)`) and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. The executable name is unchanged, and it builds with no warnings.\n\n**Timings** (default threshold of 2.0; the 128-core, 256-thread machine was shared, with load averages of about 18–42, so these are noisy):\n\n| Points | 1 thread | 8 | 32 | 256 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 20000 | 26.6 s | 3.6 s | 1.1 s | 0.58 s |\n| 50000 | 322 s | 41.5 s | 11.5 s | 3.9 s |\n\nThese figures include the algorithm changes, not just the parallelism. One weak spot: very small inputs, or ones that produce many tiny clusters, can be slower at 256 threads than at 8–32. For example, 1000 points took about 0.2 s at 256 threads versus about 10 ms at 8–32, because the per-round synchronisation costs more than the work.","ttft_ms":1921,"type":"result","duration_ms":1193638,"uuid":"0df92ba3-1bde-4e52-b81d-4824d416c115","ttft_stream_ms":942,"time_to_request_ms":140,"first_content_frame_ms":943,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
